We trace this concentration to two mechanisms in the GRPO update. At the response level, high-probability responses dominate the group gradient through repeated occurrence. At the token level, GRPO's importance ratio scales gradients, further reinforcing tokens that become more likely under the current policy. We propose ReCo, a reweighting method that addresses both effects. Response contributions are normalized by their expected occurrence within the rollout group, and the token-level importance ratio is replaced with a variance-based ratio that gives larger update scale to non-saturated decision points where alternative token choices remain plausible. Across Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B/7B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ReCo improves Pass@k for large values of k and is comparable to GRPO for small values of k.

</details>


### [179] [Actions Have Consequences: Detecting Outcome Performativity using Intervention Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26908)
*Brandon Gower-Winter,Georg Krempl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OPAB的检测方法，用于识别预测结果本身会影响其预测目标的现象（结果表演性），并通过理论分析和实验验证了该方法的可行性与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在姑息治疗、信用分配和推荐系统等领域，预测结果可能因果性地影响其预测目标（即结果表演性），但缺乏系统化的检测方法来识别这种现象。

Method: 提出了结果表演性A/B检测（OPAB）方法，通过比较不同预测分组（干预）产生的结果分布差异来检测结果表演性；推导了在不同假设类下的样本复杂度界限。

Result: 实验验证了OPAB在多种情况下能有效检测结果表演性；同时发现了"不可区分区域"，即当干预数量不足时无法检测到结果表演性。

Conclusion: OPAB为检测结果表演性提供了有效框架，但在样本稀缺、成本高昂或伦理受限的环境中检测存在挑战；在Open Bandits数据集上的案例研究验证了其有效性。

Abstract: In many domains such as Palliative Care, Credit Assignment and Recommender Systems, predictions may causally influence the outcomes they predict. This phenomena is known as Outcome Performativity. This paper formalises an approach for detecting Outcome Performativity using prediction intervention called Outcome Performativity A/B Detection (OPAB). OPAB enables the detection of Outcome Performativity by assessing the dissimilarity in outcome distributions produced by different predictions groups (interventions). If that dissimilarity is significant, Outcome Performativity is detected. We derive sample complexity bounds for OPAB under various Outcome Performative assumption classes which we empirically validate. Results show that detecting Outcome Performativity using OPAB is achievable in numerous cases. Results also show the presence of regions of indistinguishability which describe settings where the allotted number of interventions are insufficient for detecting Outcome Performativity. The results of which have broader practical implications for the detectability of Outcome Performativity in settings where samples are scarce, cost-prohibitive or potentially unethical to obtain. The paper concludes with a case study on the efficacy of OPAB on the Open Bandits dataset, and provides directions for future work.

</details>


### [180] [Two Calls Beat Five Agents: Evaluating Multi-Agent Pipelines Against Self-Refinement for Local Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26922)
*Ashish Prajapati,Om Mohite*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将多智能体LLM管道系统（Parishad）部署到本地7B模型上，发现通信格式和实现细节比架构复杂度更重要，简单方法往往能匹配或超越多智能体管道性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM管道系统主要使用大规模商业模型进行基准测试，缺乏对本地小模型部署效果的系统性研究。

Method: 将Parishad（五角色多智能体系统）部署在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上，在GSM8K和HumanEval数据集上测试，对比直接提示、两次自优化（V1）和任务感知门控重设计（V2）等多种策略。

Result: GSM8K上：JSON格式下准确率从75%降至45%（错误累积），明文格式恢复至82%，V1策略达86.2%且节省7.4倍token。HumanEval上：V1破坏性能（从96.3%降至66.5%），V2策略保持95.1%准确率。

Conclusion: 通信格式和实现细节比架构复杂度更关键；简单方法在本地7B模型部署中可匹配或超越多智能体管道系统。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipeline systems break down the task among multiple roles for better reasoning, but are benchmarked mainly with large-scale commercial models. In this study, we investigate Parishad, a structured multi-agent system involving five roles, by deploying it on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, a local model, on two datasets: GSM8K (500 questions) and HumanEval (164 questions), compared with prompting directly and two-call self-refinement. The multi-agent system drops GSM8K accuracy from 75.0\% to 45.0\% with JSON data format due to the error accumulation problem. With plaintext format, the accuracy is restored to 82.0\%. A two-call self-refinement strategy (V1) can achieve 86.2\% accuracy on GSM8K, with 7.4$\times$ lower token usage. However, the same V1 implementation on HumanEval---where direct accuracy is already 96.3\%---actively destroys performance (66.5\%). A task-aware gated redesign (V2) applied to HumanEval preserves accuracy at 95.1\%. Our results demonstrate that communication format and implementation details determine outcomes more than architectural complexity, and that simpler approaches match or outperform multi-agent pipelines for local 7B model deployment. All code and data are released.

</details>


### [181] [Temporally Centered SIGReg Improves Multi-Task LeWorldModel Learning: From Analysis to Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26924)
*Chang Liu,Fei Suo,Yanzhou Jin,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yaonan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LeWM中的SIGReg在多任务场景下会导致表征混淆，提出将其应用于时间中心化残差而非潜在边际分布，从而在LIBERO基准上显著提升性能，接近大规模预训练策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWM中的SIGReg在单任务场景表现优异，但多任务训练时下游行为克隆性能显著下降，亟需解决该问题。

Method: 将SIGReg应用于时间中心化残差，而非潜在边际分布，保留抗坍塌效果的同时避免对聚类中心分离施加直接正则化压力。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，长时域套件下游成功率提升1.7倍，四个套件平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%；无需外部预训练，略优于从零训练的Diffusion Policy，接近大规模预训练策略基线。

Conclusion: 边际高斯先验与多任务潜在结构存在结构性不兼容，所提方法为稳定可扩展的端到端多任务世界模型学习提供了简单有效的路径。

Abstract: Recent work on LeWorldModel (LeWM) has shown that the Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) enables stable end-to-end world-model learning from pixels by regularizing the latent marginal distribution toward an isotropic Gaussian, thereby preventing representation collapse. While effective and elegant in single-task settings, this recipe does not extend reliably to multi-task training, leading to substantially worse downstream behavior-cloning performance. In this paper, we show that marginal Gaussianization compresses the separation between task-dependent latent clusters relative to within-cluster variation. This compression introduces representation aliasing across tasks and states, and makes the learned representations highly sensitive to small visual perturbations. To address this problem, we apply SIGReg to temporally centered residuals rather than to the latent marginal distribution. This surrogate target places no direct regularization pressure on the separation among cluster centers, removes the requirement that the full latent follow a single isotropic Gaussian, and retains the anti-collapse effect of SIGReg. On the LIBERO benchmark, our method improves downstream success on the long-horizon suite by 1.7x and raises the average success rate across four suites from 53.2% to 73.6%. Without external pretraining, it slightly outperforms Diffusion Policy trained from scratch and approaches the performance of large-scale pretrained policy baselines. These results reveal a structural incompatibility between marginal Gaussian priors and multi-task latent structure, and provide a simple route toward stable and scalable end-to-end multi-task world-model learning.

</details>


### [182] [Surrogate assisted diversity estimation in neural ensemble search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26940)
*Alexandr Udeneev,Petr Babkin,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双目标代理引导的神经集成搜索方法，通过两个代理模型分别评估预测准确性和多样性潜力，高效找到既个体强又集体多样的架构组合，在多个数据集上达到与Deep Ensembles等基线相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经集成搜索（NES）方法计算成本高昂，且联合优化个体架构与集成组合会导致搜索空间指数级增长，计算上难以处理。

Method: 将候选架构表示为有向无环图，训练两个独立的代理模型分别估计预测准确性和多样性潜力，结合两者的估计来引导NES框架高效搜索。

Result: 在FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，该方法的最终集成性能优于或与Deep Ensembles、Random Search等标准基线相当。

Conclusion: 双目标代理引导方法能有效解决NES的计算复杂性问题，在保证集成质量的同时降低搜索代价。

Abstract: Ensembles are a standard way to improve the performance and robustness of deep neural networks, but their effectiveness crucially depends on both the quality and the diversity of individual models. Most neural architecture search (NAS) methods are computationally expensive. Extending them to neural ensemble search (NES), which requires joint optimization of individual architectures and their ensemble composition, leads to an exponential growth of the search space and makes the problem computationally intractable. To address this, we introduce a dual-objective surrogate-guided ensemble search: candidate architectures are represented as directed acyclic graphs, and two surrogate models are trained independently to estimate predictive accuracy and diversity potential. Their combined estimates guide an NES framework that efficiently identifies architectures that are both individually strong and collectively diverse. Our final ensemble achieves competitive or superior performance compared to standard baselines such as Deep Ensembles and Random Search on FashionMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100.

</details>


### [183] [Foundation Models for Face Presentation Attack Detection: A Unified Linear-Probing Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26993)
*Peter Lorenz,Anjith George,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了24种冻结的预训练视觉基础模型（包括自监督ViT、视觉-语言编码器和监督CNN）在人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的表现，使用线性探测协议在MCIO基准上进行测试。结果表明：冻结模型在数据集内表现良好，但跨数据集泛化能力不足；模型规模有益但非单调；InternViT-6B在数据集内误差最低，CLIP ViT-B/32在跨数据集迁移-计算权衡上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）在跨数据集评估中面临域偏移挑战，且大规模标注数据稀缺。作者希望探究通用预训练视觉基础模型是否已经编码了与PAD相关的信息，仅需少量任务特定训练即可利用。

Method: 采用统一的线性探测协议，冻结24种预训练编码器（包括自监督ViT、视觉-语言编码器和监督CNN）的骨干网络，仅训练一个轻量级线性分类头，在MCIO基准（MSU-MFSD、CASIA-FASD、Replay-Attack、OULU-NPU）上进行数据集内和跨数据集评估。

Result: 冻结的基础模型表示在数据集内PAD任务上表现强劲，但跨数据集性能不可靠；模型规模在部分族内有帮助但非单调；InternViT-6B取得最低平均数据集内错误率，CLIP ViT-B/32在跨数据集迁移与计算效率的权衡上最优。

Conclusion: 预训练表示确实包含PAD相关信息，但仅靠线性探测不足以解决域偏移问题，显式的域适应仍然是必要的。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) remains challenging under cross-dataset evaluation, where domain shift degrades models trained on a single dataset. The scarcity of large-scale labeled data motivates adapting pretrained vision models rather than training task-specific architectures from scratch, raising a fundamental question: do general-purpose vision foundation models encode PAD-relevant information accessible with minimal task-specific training? To investigate, we systematically evaluate 24 frozen encoders, including self-supervised vision transformers, vision-language encoders, and supervised CNNs, using a unified linear-probing protocol on the MCIO benchmark (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, OULU-NPU). The backbone remains fixed, and only a lightweight linear head is trained to isolate the PAD information already present in the pretrained representation. %We report intra- and cross-dataset performance, along with accuracy-compute trade-offs, relative to two specialist PAD baselines. Results show that frozen foundation-model representations can support strong intra-dataset PAD performance with only a linear classifier, but this performance does not reliably transfer across datasets. Model scale is beneficial within several families, although the effect is not monotonic and is strongly mediated by architecture and pretraining. InternViT-6B achieves the lowest mean intra-dataset error, whereas CLIP ViT-B/32 offers the most favorable cross-dataset transfer-compute trade-off among the evaluated probes. These findings suggest that while pretrained representations contain PAD-relevant information, explicit adaptation remains necessary to address domain shift.

</details>


### [184] [What Can Latent World Models Know? Physical Parameter Identifiability in Multimodal Predictive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27017)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过POKEWORLD和RH20T实验证明，潜在世界模型中的物理参数学习由预测目标决定，而非仅仅依赖输入信号。只有被预测目标直接需要的物理量（如接触刚度）才会被有效编码，且数据量增加仅能改善已习得的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究潜在世界模型中，模型学到的物理量是由什么因素决定的——是输入数据本身，还是预测目标的选择？

Method: 在POKEWORLD环境中进行受控干预实验，使用证书门控协议验证参数的可恢复性，通过对比不同预测目标（单步预测、多模态预测等）下潜变量对质量、阻力、接触刚度等物理参数的编码效果。

Result: 1) 接触刚度只有预测触觉时才进入潜变量(R²=0.50 vs -0.02)；2) 单步预测下视觉潜变量丢弃了完全可见的物体状态；3) 阻力在所有确定性预测目标下表现极差(0.13)，但监督头可达0.45；4) RH20T验证了两种机制在机器人数据上的普适性。

Conclusion: 目标函数的结构决定了潜变量习得哪些物理参数，增加数据量只能改善已习得的参数，无法帮助模型学习未被目标要求的新参数。

Abstract: A central premise of latent world models is that predicting the future forces a representation to internalize the physics of its environment. Which physical quantities does a trained latent actually contain, and what decides this? We answer with controlled interventions in POKEWORLD, an interactive environment whose visually identical objects hide mass, drag, and contact stiffness. A certificate-gated protocol first certifies each parameter as recoverable from raw observations, then measures whether it enters the latent, so a null result can be attributed to the objective rather than to the environment. The resulting identifiability map has two organizing mechanisms and one frontier. Inputs limit what can be known, while prediction targets decide what is retained. Stiffness enters the latent only when touch is forecast ($R^2=0.50$, compared with $-0.02$ when the same signal is merely fused into the input), and under single-step prediction a vision-only latent discards even perfectly visible object state. Drag marks the frontier. It carries a recoverability certificate of 0.89 yet plateaus near 0.13 under every deterministic prediction objective we test, while a supervised head on the same trunk reaches 0.45. Parameters whose readout is slow and ratio-type under the sensed coordinates fall outside what these objectives acquire. On RH20T, an input-target factorial across scaling curves reproduces both mechanisms across two robots and 4,258 episodes. Every arm missing information or prediction pressure stays flat over a fivefold data range, and only the full multimodal objective forecasts force beyond a persistence baseline, with held-out gains that grow with scale. Objective structure determines which physical parameters a latent acquires, and additional data improves only the parameters it already acquires.

</details>


### [185] [BayesAME: Bayesian Active Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27023)
*Paula Cordero Encinar,Taylan Cemgil,Arnaud Doucet,Virginia Aglietti,Silvia Chiappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BayesAME，一个基于序贯贝叶斯框架的方法，能够自动确定基准测试中的核心子集大小，通过建模性能不确定性并迭代扩展核心子集，直到性能估计波动和不确定性低于用户定义的阈值，从而在保证估计可靠性的前提下减少评估成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型评估中，完整跑完所有基准测试耗时且昂贵，现有方法通常需要用户手动指定核心子集大小，无法在保证性能估计可靠性的前提下自动确定合适的子集规模。

Method: 提出BayesAME框架：1) 将模型性能建模为随机变量，为具有相同历史性能的项目组定义潜在能力；2) 使用联合先验分布编码目标模型与历史模型的相似性；3) 基于后验分布推导性能估计器、量化不确定性，并通过信息增益准则选择项目加入核心子集；4) 迭代扩展直到性能估计波动和不确定性低于阈值；5) 提出多目标扩展以利用目标模型间的性能相关性。

Result: 在多种基准测试上的实验表明，BayesAME一致优于现有方法的序贯适配版；证实非随机核心子集选择优于随机选择，回应了文献中的质疑；利用连续响应对数似然比传统二元评分能显著提升估计精度。

Conclusion: BayesAME为自动确定核心子集大小提供了有效方案，能够在保证性能估计可靠性的前提下最小化评估成本，并验证了非随机子集选择和连续评分的重要性。

Abstract: Evaluating large generative models across benchmarks is time-consuming and computationally expensive. This drives the need for methods that can estimate full benchmark performance by evaluating models on only a subset of items, known as a coreset. Current literature mostly requires the practitioner to input a coreset size. However, when reliable performance estimation takes priority over efficiency, an evaluation method should also be capable of automatically determining a coreset size that reflects this priority. We introduce BayesAME, a sequential Bayesian framework specifically targeting automatic determination of the coreset size. BayesAME models performance as a random variable by defining a latent ability for each group of items sharing the same historical model performances, with a joint prior distribution encoding the belief that the target model behaves similarly to these historical models. The posterior distribution over these abilities is used to derive performance estimators, quantify performance uncertainty, and select items to add to the coreset via an information-gain criterion. The coreset is iteratively augmented until the performance estimate fluctuation and the performance uncertainty fall below their respective user-defined thresholds. We propose a multi-target extension that captures performance correlations across multiple target models to further reduce the coreset size. Through extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that BayesAME consistently outperforms sequential adaptations of existing methods. Crucially, our comprehensive analysis addresses recent skepticism in the literature, establishing that non-random coreset selection is advantageous over random selection. Finally, we highlight that leveraging continuous response log-likelihoods over traditional binary scores significantly enhances estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [186] [TreeCCA: Canonical Correlation Analysis via Gradient-Boosted Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27027)
*James Chapman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeCCA是首个将梯度提升树作为CCA编码器的方法，通过Eckart-Young损失实现端到端训练，兼具非线性精度与原生可解释性，在多视图表格数据上性能优于或媲美Deep CCA，且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型相关分析（CCA）长期依赖线性或神经网络编码器，而梯度提升树（GBT）在表格数据中占据主导地位，现有方法未能将GBT的强大能力引入CCA框架。

Method: 提出TreeCCA方法，利用Eckart-Young（EY）损失函数提供每个样本的闭式梯度，直接作为自定义目标函数集成到标准GBT库（如XGBoost、LightGBM）中，实现端到端训练。

Result: 在合成基准上匹配或超越Deep CCA（Signed Power: 2.61 vs 2.43；Hermite: 2.93 vs 2.89）；在零线性交叉协方差稀疏基准上，Precision@S=1.00（p=50），而PMD无法检测信号；UCI HAR传感器融合任务中以5倍更低成本达到可比精度。

Conclusion: TreeCCA是首个结合非线性精度与原生可解释性的CCA方法，无需架构设计、超参数熟悉且默认参数性能强劲，为多视图表格数据提供了一种可靠、高效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Gradient-boosted trees dominate tabular machine learning, yet canonical correlation analysis has always relied on linear or neural encoders. We propose \textbf{TreeCCA}, the first method to train gradient-boosted tree ensembles end-to-end as CCA encoders, inheriting their plug-and-play reliability: no architecture design, familiar hyperparameters, and strong performance with defaults. The technical enabler is the Eckart-Young (EY) loss, which supplies closed-form per-sample gradients that slot directly into any standard GBT library (XGBoost, LightGBM) as a custom objective.
  TreeCCA is the first CCA method to combine nonlinear accuracy with native interpretability: every tree split selects one feature, so gain importances reveal which inputs drive cross-view correlation at no extra cost. We demonstrate these properties on synthetic benchmarks, where TreeCCA matches or exceeds Deep CCA (2.61 vs.\ 2.43 on Signed Power; 2.93 vs.\ 2.89 on Hermite), and on a sparse benchmark with zero linear cross-view covariance, where TreeCCA recovers the true support with $\text{Precision@}S = 1.00$ at $p=50$ while PMD finds no signal. On the UCI HAR sensor-fusion benchmark, TreeCCA achieves comparable accuracy to Deep CCA at $5\times$ lower cost, while XGBoost gain importances directly validate a physics-motivated hypothesis about the data --- an interpretation not readily available with neural encoders. Across five popular tabular multi-view datasets, TreeMCCA consistently matches or exceeds linear CCA in both nonlinear correlation extraction and downstream classification accuracy.

</details>


### [187] [CoCaRS: Correlation Calibration-Based Redundancy Suppression for Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27054)
*Fengming Yu,Haiwei Pan,Kejia Zhang,Chunling Chen,Jian Guan,Baoying Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCaRS方法，通过相关性校准（CEE和SAC）和自适应系数调节（ACR）改进异构知识蒸馏中的冗余抑制，在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构知识蒸馏中，冗余抑制方法存在两个问题：1）均匀去相关会削弱有用的结构信息；2）固定系数对师生模型对和训练阶段敏感，影响效果。

Method: 提出CoCaRS框架，包含三个核心模块：1）Confusion Evidence Estimation (CEE) 捕捉可靠语义关系用于相关性估计；2）Strength Allocation Control (SAC) 在去相关过程中保留判别性结构；3）Adaptive Coefficient Regulation (ACR) 根据相对损失规模自适应调节冗余抑制目标的贡献。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K数据集上的实验表明，CoCaRS有效提升了蒸馏性能，并降低了对系数设置的敏感性。

Conclusion: CoCaRS通过校准特征去相关和自适应系数调节，解决了异构知识蒸馏中冗余抑制的结构信息损失和系数敏感性问题，是一种有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) enables a compact student model to learn from a powerful teacher and has become an effective paradigm for model compression. The emergence of diverse model architectures has extended KD from homogeneous to heterogeneous settings. However, differences in architectural inductive biases between the teacher and student models often result in substantial representation discrepancies, limiting the effectiveness of direct knowledge transfer. Recently, redundancy suppression has offered a new perspective on heterogeneous KD by preserving cross-architecture invariance and reducing feature redundancy through decorrelation of teacher-student feature correlations. Nevertheless, this formulation may weaken useful structural information through uniform decorrelation, while a fixed coefficient may make the effective contribution of redundancy suppression sensitive to teacher-student pairs and training stages. To address these problems, Correlation Calibration-based Redundancy Suppression (CoCaRS) is proposed to better retain structural information while suppressing redundancy and reduce sensitivity to coefficient settings across teacher-student pairs and training stages. Specifically, CoCaRS calibrates feature decorrelation through Confusion Evidence Estimation (CEE) and Strength Allocation Control (SAC), which respectively capture reliable semantic relations for correlation estimation and preserve discriminative structure during decorrelation. Adaptive Coefficient Regulation (ACR) further regulates the contribution of the calibrated redundancy suppression objective according to its relative loss scale, reducing sensitivity to coefficient settings. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1K validate the effectiveness of CoCaRS in improving distillation performance and reducing sensitivity to coefficient settings. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [188] [Lottery Tickets Are Not Deployment Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27031)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定下游决策逻辑的实际部署场景中，稀疏模型（包括彩票票据）能否无缝替换密集模型。实验表明，尽管稀疏模型能恢复密集模型的准确率，但在行为兼容性上存在显著差异，导致下游决策发生7%-10%的改变，从而增加了重新配置和验证的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献关于稀疏化、压缩和彩票票据对模型行为的影响结论不一，且未考虑实际部署中决策逻辑已固定的情况。为从实用角度评估这些矛盾结果，本文研究了生产环境下的替换问题。

Method: 在部署层面研究准确率匹配的彩票票据或其他稀疏模型能否替换密集模型而无需重新配置下游决策逻辑。通过审计校准、OOD响应、类别级可靠性、表征和下游策略决策等行为，用行为兼容性距离总结偏差。

Result: 稀疏候选模型能恢复密集参考模型的准确率，但行为上存在差异；彩票票据在部分设置中显示出更低的污染准确率；在固定阈值策略诊断中，彩票票据替换导致7%-10%的接受-审查决策发生改变。

Conclusion: 建立了干净准确率认证的局限性：与固定模型建立兼容性不同于将变化归因于稀疏性或将所有偏差视为有害。理论解释了即使点态top-1一致也无法约束固定阈值决策变化，决策边界附近的置信度变化会产生一阶路由变化。

Abstract: Reports on how sparsification, compression, and lottery tickets change model behavior have been mixed in the prior literature, with beneficial effects observed in some studies and adverse effects in others. Moreover, prior work has not considered actual deployment conditions, where decision logic is already fixed for the incumbent. To assess these mixed findings from a practical standpoint, we study the production-replacement question at the deployment level, namely whether an accuracy-matched lottery ticket or another sparse challenger can replace an incumbent dense model without reconfiguring downstream decision logic. We therefore audit a broad, protocol-specific panel of deployment-relevant behaviors spanning calibration, OOD response, class-level reliability, representations, and downstream policy decisions, and summarize clean-accuracy-excluded deviations with a behavioral-compatibility distance. Across extensive experiments, sparse candidates repeatedly recover dense-reference accuracy yet remain behaviorally different; in several study-band-matched settings, LTs also show lower corruption accuracy. In small-gap settings with fixed-threshold policy diagnostics, lottery-ticket replacement changes 7% to 10% of accept--review decisions. This churn creates precisely the burden that drop-in replacement is meant to avoid: reconfiguring and revalidating downstream decision logic. These findings establish the limits of clean-accuracy certification: Establishing compatibility with a fixed incumbent is distinct from attributing churn uniquely to sparsity or treating every measured deviation as harmful. Our theory explains the routing result: Even exact pointwise top-1 agreement cannot bound fixed-threshold decision changes, and small confidence shifts near the operating boundary can generate first-order routing churn.

</details>


### [189] [Parameter-Free Dynamic Regret for Online Convex Optimization under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27073)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HT-PAder算法，首次实现了重尾噪声下非平稳在线凸优化的无参数通用动态遗憾，并证明了其最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重尾噪声（仅有限p阶中心矩）的非平稳环境中，静态遗憾已有较好研究，但实现无参数且通用的动态遗憾仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 结合重启AdaGrad专家（几何块长度池）与路径级元算法AdaGrad-Hedge（无需对元损失施加矩条件），组成HT-PAder算法。

Result: HT-PAder实现了期望通用动态遗憾为Õ(GD√(T(1+P_T/D)) + σD T^(1/p) (1+P_T/D)^((p-1)/p))，且无需预知任何问题参数。在p=2时首次得到无参数极小极大通用动态遗憾保证，并给出匹配的下界。

Conclusion: HT-PAder解决了重尾噪声下非平稳OCO中无参数通用动态遗憾的关键问题，且达到了路径长度指数的最优性。

Abstract: We study online convex optimization (OCO) in non-stationary environments under heavy-tailed noise, where the stochastic gradient oracle admits only a finite $p$-th central moment for some $p \in (1, 2]$. While static regret is well-understood, achieving universal dynamic regret in a parameter-free manner remains an open challenge. We resolve this by proposing \textbf{HT-PAder}, a parameter-free algorithm combining restarted AdaGrad experts over a geometric pool of block lengths with a pathwise meta-algorithm, \textbf{AdaGrad-Hedge}, which requires no moment conditions on meta-losses. For a domain of diameter $D$, Lipschitz constant $G$, noise level $σ$, and comparator path length $P_T$, HT-PAder achieves an expected universal dynamic regret of \[ \widetilde O\left( GD\sqrt{T(1+P_T/D)} + σD T^{1/p}(1+P_T/D)^{(p-1)/p} \right). \] The algorithm does not require prior knowledge of any of these problem parameters. Even in the special case of finite variance ($p=2$), HT-PAder provides the first parameter-free minimax universal dynamic regret guarantee. We also prove a matching lower bound, establishing the optimality of the path-length exponent.

</details>


### [190] [Single-Beat Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation Using Ear-PPG and ECG with a Lightweight Hybrid Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27076)
*Kindeep K. Dhatt,Tengyue Wu,Hanbang Hua,Yayun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级多模态可穿戴框架，通过单拍级ECG与PPG融合及混合学习架构（CNN+LightGBM），在动态条件下实现连续无袖带血压监测，MAE分别为4.02±0.21 mmHg（收缩压）和1.79±0.05 mmHg（舒张压），相比基线模型联合MAE降低28.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续无袖带血压监测受运动伪影、生理变异性及脉冲传输时间模型鲁棒性不足的限制，且多数方法依赖多秒时间窗口，在真实动态监测中信号易受间歇性干扰，亟需一种无需长时上下文的单拍级血压估计方法。

Method: 集成胸部ECG与耳夹反射PPG（均配备6轴惯性测量单元），采用混合学习架构：一维CNN从单拍PPG中提取64维嵌入，融合30个生理特征（包括PTT统计量和心率变异性），再通过LightGBM回归进行血压估计。

Result: 在10名受试者的多阶段压力协议和PulseDB公共数据集上，经30次独立运行，模型收缩压MAE为4.02±0.21 mmHg，舒张压MAE为1.79±0.05 mmHg，联合MAE相比基线模型降低28.2%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了无需长时上下文的单拍级血压估计，计算高效，适合资源受限的可穿戴设备部署，代码已开源。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring remains challenging due to motion artifacts, physiological variability, and the limited robustness of conventional pulse transit time (PTT) models under dynamic conditions. Many prior approaches rely on multi-second windows to stabilize estimation, an assumption that is frequently violated during real-world monitoring with intermittent signal corruption. Here, we show that discriminative BP-related information is preserved at the single-beat level and present a lightweight multi-modal wearable framework for continuous BP estimation. The system integrates synchronized chest electrocardiography (ECG) and ear-clip reflectance photoplethysmography, each co-located with a 6-axis inertial measurement unit to provide motion context. We introduce a hybrid learning architecture in which a one-dimensional convolutional neural network extracts a 64-dimensional embedding from individual PPG beats and fuses it with 30 physiology-grounded features, including PTT statistics and heart rate variability, followed by LightGBM regression. The method was evaluated using a multi-phase stress protocol ($n=10$) and the PulseDB public dataset with subject-disjoint validation. Across 30 independent runs, the model achieved mean absolute errors of $4.02 \pm 0.21$~mmHg for systolic BP and $1.79 \pm 0.05$~mmHg for diastolic BP, corresponding to a 28.2\% reduction in combined MAE relative to baseline models. By enabling beat-wise estimation without long temporal context, this framework supports computationally efficient cuffless BP monitoring suitable for wearable deployment under practical resource constraints. The source code for this work is available at https://github.com/SYMBIOX-Lab/BP-wireless.

</details>


### [191] [Equilibrium Training of Energy-Based Models with Parallel Trajectory Tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27077)
*Nicolas Béreux,Aurélien Decelle,Cyril Furtlehner,Beatriz Seoane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于并行轨迹回火(PTT)的EBM训练算法，通过利用优化路径的连续性实现平衡采样，在数据稀缺和多模态科学数据集上实现稳定高效训练，计算成本与持久对比散度相当，并在RBM和离散表格数据上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量基模型(EBM)在科学数据生成建模中具有可解释性，但马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛混合不良严重限制了其可靠性和实用性。

Method: 提出并行轨迹回火(PTT)训练算法，结合储层采样和自适应优化，利用优化路径连续性在训练过程中维持平衡采样。

Result: PTT在受限玻尔兹曼机上持续优于现有EBM训练方法；在离散表格数据上超越最先进的深度生成模型，生成更高质量样本，对过拟合和有限数据更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PTT使得EBM的平衡最大似然训练变得实用且计算高效，同时还能直接估计热化时间、提供平衡样本和准确的对数似然。

Abstract: Energy-Based Models (EBMs) provide an interpretable framework for generative modeling of scientific data, but poor Markov Chain Monte Carlo mixing often limits their reliability. We introduce a training algorithm based on Parallel Trajectory Tempering (PTT), which exploits the continuity of the optimization path to maintain equilibrium sampling throughout learning. This enables stable and fast training on highly multimodal and data-scarce scientific datasets. Combined with reservoir sampling and adaptive optimization, PTT has a computational cost comparable to Persistent Contrastive Divergence, making it a practical replacement for standard training methods. It also provides direct estimates of thermalization times, equilibrium samples from trained models, and accurate log-likelihoods at essentially no additional cost. Experiments on Restricted Boltzmann Machines show that PTT consistently outperforms existing EBM training approaches. On discrete tabular data, it also surpasses state-of-the-art deep generative models, yielding higher-quality samples and greater robustness to overfitting and limited data. Our results make equilibrium maximum-likelihood training of EBMs practical and computationally efficient.

</details>


### [192] [Scores Are Not Decisions: Cost-Aware Stopping for Tool Acquisition in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27083)
*Yicheng Feng,Yan Zhang,Yan Cheng,Wei Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAM-DF框架，在异构成本下决定工具选择的最优终止点，在减少37%工具使用量的同时保持任务成功率，是轻量级预执行插件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖多种外部服务时面临工具选择的根本挑战：获取工具太少导致信息不足，太多则增加成本、上下文负载和隐私暴露。现有排序方法无法确定应该选择多少个工具，且未解决异构成本下的获取决策问题。

Method: 将工具选择问题建模为成本感知的边际决策聚焦停止（CAM-DF），在排序后的工具前缀上进行决策。训练目标基于当前停止与最优继续之间的离线差距：符号标记决策方向，幅度加权每个错误的收益损失。证明该目标与停止目标贝叶斯对齐，并证明异构成本下纯评分规则是次优的。

Result: 在五个工具使用领域的1,343个任务上评估。在τ-bench Retail中，CAM-DF在所有可部署方法中获得最高收益，在所有五种排序来源和两种成本机制下均优于预测-阈值基线。在异构成本和高成本压力下达到最优，在弱排序下收益更大。实机执行中比完全访问减少37%工具暴露，同时保持任务成功率。

Conclusion: CAM-DF系列是轻量级预执行插件，可将现有工具排序转化为更低成本的获取决策，无需微调底层LLM。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly depend on diverse external services such as search engines, databases, and connectors, agent harnesses face a fundamental tool-selection challenge: acquiring too few tools leaves the task under-informed, while too many adds cost, context load, and privacy exposure. Routers and retrievers can rank candidate tools by relevance, but a ranking alone does not determine how many are worth selecting. Existing approaches leave acquisition under heterogeneous costs unaddressed. We formulate this decision as cost-aware marginal decision-focused stopping (CAM-DF) over ranked tool prefixes, with CAM-DF-lite as a compact interpretable variant. We train directly on the offline gap between stopping now and the best continuation: its sign labels the decision, its magnitude weights each error by the payoff at stake. We prove this objective is Bayes-aligned with the stopping target and that score-only rules are suboptimal under heterogeneous costs. We evaluate on 1,343 tasks across five tool-use domains. On $τ$-bench Retail, CAM-DF attains the highest payoff among deployable methods, with gains over a predict-then-threshold baseline across all five ranking sources and two cost regimes. Our approach is state-of-the-art under heterogeneous costs and high cost pressure, with larger gains under weaker rankings. In live execution, CAM-DF exposes the agent to 37\% fewer tools than full access while maintaining comparable task success. The CAM-DF family is a lightweight pre-execution plugin that turns existing tool rankings into lower-cost acquisition decisions without fine-tuning the underlying LLM.

</details>


### [193] [Sky sphere representation in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27092)
*Aleksandr Berdnikov,Yevgeny Liokumovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现约100B规模的语言模型在残差流中编码了可解码的夜空星图表示，该表示呈弯曲高维不可约特征流形，中位角度误差低至12°-21°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型是否在内部表征中编码了天文知识（夜空星图），并验证这种表示是否为弯曲的高维不可约特征流形。

Method: 对约100B参数的开源语言模型残差流进行解码分析，使用LOO测试评估表示质量，通过主成分分析观察星图结构的浮现，并验证表示并非简单相关性泄漏。

Result: 大多数开源模型在残差流中存在可解码的夜空表示；该表示在LOO测试中显著，R²评分达65-85%，中位角度误差12°-21°；且排除了简单相关性泄漏的可能。

Conclusion: 首次发现语言模型中存在弯曲的高维不可约特征流形（夜空星图表示），表明大模型不仅在语义层面编码知识，还可能形成几何结构化的内部表征。

Abstract: We analyze whether language models of size ~100B have a representation of the night sky map that is decodable from their residual stream. We find that most of the considered open-source models do have such a representation, and it often even surfaces to the top principal components on prompts that ask questions like ``what is close to this object in the night sky''. In all but one model this representation showed significant scores in LOO testing, containing up to 65-85% of variance ($R^2$-score) and having median angular error down to $12^\circ-21^\circ$. We verify that our representation is not a simple leak from a correlated flat representation. To our knowledge, this representation is the first example of a curved high-dimensional irreducible feature manifold.
  Codes used in the paper are published at https://github.com/l3erdnik/Decodable-sky

</details>


### [194] [Cost-Sensitive Conformal Prediction and Human-in-the-Loop Abstention for Imbalanced High-Stakes Decision Support: A Multi-Domain Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27143)
*Manpreet Singh,Akshatha Srikantha,Shyamal Lakhanpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准边际共形预测在严重类别不平衡和高风险决策场景下对少数类覆盖不足的问题，提出Mondrian CP结合成本控制弃权机制可显著提升少数类覆盖并降低决策成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险决策系统（如信用评分、欺诈检测、医疗诊断）面临严重的类别不平衡和非对称错误成本，标准边际共形预测无法为稀有的少数类提供可靠的置信区间，导致其覆盖率极低（低至0.5%），亟需更有效的解决方案。

Method: 在15个真实不平衡表格数据集上，使用7种分类模型、3种概率校准技术和10个随机种子，进行了共3,150次实验运行，系统比较了边际CP、类别条件（Mondrian）CP和成本控制弃权机制的性能。

Result: Mondrian CP将少数类覆盖率平均提升了61.7个百分点（p < 1e-80），恢复了对少数类的有效覆盖。结合成本控制弃权后，在合理的人工审核预算下，预期决策成本显著低于标准决策边界、基于置信度的拒绝器和风险控制拒绝器。

Conclusion: 研究量化了数据集特定的盈亏平衡阈值，为在高风险决策支持系统中部署无分布假设、成本感知的不确定性量化方法提供了实用指导。

Abstract: High-stakes decision systems in credit scoring, fraud detection, healthcare, and industrial safety require reliable uncertainty quantification under severe class imbalance and asymmetric error costs. Standard marginal conformal prediction (CP) provides valid overall coverage guarantees; however, we show that it severely under-covers rare, costly minority classes, with minority-class coverage dropping to as low as 0.5% on certain datasets. To characterize and address this limitation, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark comparing marginal CP, class-conditional (Mondrian) CP, and cost-controlled abstention mechanisms across 15 real-world imbalanced tabular datasets, 7 classification models, 3 probability calibration techniques, and 10 random seeds, resulting in 3,150 experimental runs. Our results show that Mondrian CP restores valid minority-class coverage, achieving an average minority-coverage improvement of 61.7 percentage points over marginal CP (p < 1e-80). Furthermore, combining Mondrian CP with cost-controlled abstention significantly reduces expected decision cost compared with standard decision boundaries, confidence-based rejectors, and risk-controlled rejectors under realistic human review budgets. We further quantify dataset-specific break-even thresholds at which deferring ambiguous instances to human experts becomes cost-effective. These findings provide practical guidance for deploying distribution-free, cost-aware uncertainty quantification in high-stakes decision support systems.

</details>


### [195] [Hierarchical Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Coherent Emergency Department Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27106)
*Filipa Lino,Bárbara Tavares,Carlos Santiago,Cláudia Soares,Manuel Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HierSTT，一个基于Transformer的分层框架，用于同时预测医院、地区和全国三个层面的急诊需求，解决了传统单层预测的不一致性问题。在葡萄牙81家医院的真实数据集上，相比最佳非分层深度学习基线，平均WAPE降低32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊部门面临患者需求不可预测、季节性激增和非紧急就诊等多重压力，而现有方法仅在单一层面独立预测急诊需求，忽略了医院-地区-国家之间的层级关系，导致预测结果在跨层面汇总时不一致。

Method: 提出HierSTT分层Transformer框架：使用时间融合Transformer捕捉国家级动态；时空Transformer编码器-解码器模块分别建模地区和医院层面的需求（条件于高层预测）；引入一致性感知损失函数在训练中惩罚跨层面不一致性。

Result: 在葡萄牙81家医院、5个地区卫生管理部门的数据集上，HierSTT相比最佳非分层深度学习基线平均WAPE降低32%，优于所有经典分层协调方法，并生成近乎一致的跨层面预测。

Conclusion: HierSTT有效解决了多层级急诊需求预测中的不一致性问题，为医疗机构、地区协调和国家层面的容量规划提供了统一的预测框架。

Abstract: Emergency Departments (EDs) are critical access points in healthcare systems, yet they face persistent pressure from unpredictable patient demand, seasonal surges, and non-urgent visits. Effective ED planning requires forecasts at multiple decision-making levels: hospitals need local demand estimates for staffing and bed management, regions require forecasts to coordinate healthcare units, and national authorities need system-wide projections for capacity planning. However, most existing approaches forecast ED demand independently at a single level, ignoring the hierarchy linking hospitals, regions, and national systems. This can produce incoherent predictions, where hospital-level forecasts do not aggregate consistently to regional or national demand. We propose HierSTT, a hierarchical Transformer-based framework for coherent multi-level ED forecasting. HierSTT jointly predicts hospital, regional, and national level demand in a single end-to-end model. A Temporal Fusion Transformer captures national dynamics, while spatio-temporal Transformer encoder-decoder modules model regional and hospital demand conditioned on higher-level forecasts. A coherence-aware loss penalizes cross-level inconsistencies during training. We further introduce a nationwide Portuguese ED dataset covering 81 hospitals across 5 regional health administrations, with heterogeneous covariates at each level. Experiments show that HierSTT reduces average WAPE by 32\% relative to the best non-hierarchical deep learning baseline and outperforms all classical hierarchical reconciliation methods, while producing near-coherent predictions across levels. Additional resources associated with this work are available at https://github.com/FilipaLino/HierSTT.

</details>


### [196] [Voronoi Histograms for Adaptive Vectorization of Expected Persistence Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27126)
*Kaifeng Zhang,Kai Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Voronoi图的直方图方法对期望持续图（EPD）进行向量化，避免了传统方法依赖预定义光滑点变换的局限性，并在分离与归一化条件下建立了稳定性界，验证了其在分类和降维任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续图（PD）能有效捕捉点云拓扑结构，但计算复杂度高；期望持续图（EPD）通过研究多个子集的拓扑来降低时间成本，但现有EPD向量化方法大多依赖预定义的点变换（如高斯函数或景观函数），缺乏自适应性。

Method: 提出基于Voronoi图的直方图作为EPD的向量化表示，用自适应划分的计数代替光滑函数逼近，并在分离与归一化条件下分析其稳定性及Wasserstein尺度变化的保持特性。

Result: 在具有显著拓扑特征的真实数据集上，该表示在分类和降维任务中表现出有效性。

Conclusion: Voronoi直方图提供了一种无需显式光滑点变换模型的EPD向量化替代方案，在保持拓扑信息的同时具备理论稳定性保证。

Abstract: Persistence Diagram (PD) is known to capture point cloud topology effectively, but its computation has high time complexity. Expected Persistence Diagram (EPD) has been developed to reduce the time cost by studying the topology of multiple subsets of a point cloud and it serves as a distribution of topological features. Existing EPD vectorizations often rely on predefined point transformations, such as Gaussian or landscape functions. We study an alternative discretization based on Voronoi histograms, which trades smooth functional approximation for adaptive partition-based counting. We propose to use Voronoi Diagram-based histogram as the vectorization of EPD, without imposing an explicit smooth point transformation model. Under stated separation and normalization conditions, we establish stability bounds and characterize when the histogram representation preserves Wasserstein-scale variation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed representation on real-world datasets which have significant topological features for classification and dimensionality reduction tasks.

</details>


### [197] [Minimal Markovization via Stable Quotients in Holonomy-Cover Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27132)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Yongshan Chen,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)，提出了一种名为"稳定商"的最小马尔可夫充分统计量，并据此构建了Holonomy Memory强化学习框架，在实验中实现了精确的状态压缩和优于基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境下，智能体需要维护一个可递归更新的历史统计量来恢复马尔可夫性质，但最小的此类统计量通常未知。本文旨在为holonomy-cover决策过程这一结构化POMDP类刻画这一最小马尔可夫充分统计量。

Method: 构建稳定商（最粗略的保持一步奖励和商后继的观测层面抽象），证明当前观测与稳定类配对构成精确的有限马尔可夫状态；在可达性和成对决策分离条件下，证明最小记忆符号数的必要性；提出Holonomy Memory强化学习框架，通过有序边传输更新稳定类，在可重置诊断时进行最近原型类推断，并通过校准-重启归约将有限MDP保证转移到恢复状态。

Result: 实验从原始状态恢复出精确的商状态压缩，使用三个决策时记忆状态实现了完美的成对顺序准确率，匹配商预言机，并优于非预言机基线。

Conclusion: 稳定商是holonomy-cover决策过程的最小马尔可夫充分统计量，Holonomy Memory RL框架能够有效恢复这一状态并取得优异性能。

Abstract: An agent acting under partial observability must retain a recursively updateable statistic of history that restores the Markov property, but the smallest such statistic is generally unknown. We characterize this minimal Markov sufficient statistic for holonomy-cover decision processes, a structured POMDP class in which the visible dynamics are Markov and every realized visible transition applies a fixed permutation to a hidden mode. In particular, we construct the stable quotient, the coarsest observation-wise abstraction preserving one-step rewards and quotient successors, and prove that the pair of the current observation and stable class forms an exact finite Markov state. When the current class is correctly initialized, exact class tracking requires exactly the minimal memory symbols, in the sense that under reachability and pairwise decision separation at a maximizing observation, no arbitrary finite-memory controller can use fewer. Under resettable diagnostics, nearest-prototype class inference has exponentially decaying error, and a calibrate-then-restart reduction transfers finite-MDP guarantees to the recovered state. The results enable \emph{Holonomy Memory Reinforcement Learning}. It represents memory by the current stable class, updates it through ordered edge transports, identifies local class coordinates when diagnostics are available, and applies a standard finite-MDP RL backbone after synchronization. Experiments recover an exact compression from raw states to quotient states and achieve perfect paired-order accuracy with three decision-time memory states, matching the quotient oracle and outperforming the non-oracle baselines.

</details>


### [198] [Skillful forecasting of offshore winds from satellite scatterometer constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27152)
*Francesco Pinto,Luca Lanzilao,Paco Lopez Dekker,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WindCastNet，首个基于卫星散射计观测的海上风能短期预报框架，利用部分卷积LSTM网络处理时空不规则卫星数据，在北海区域相比HARMONIE MEPS模型在1小时和2小时预报时效分别降低RMSE 23%和7%，证明了卫星星座可提供独立且具有竞争力的短期海上风预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海上风能预报主要依赖数值天气预报（NWP），但NWP在分钟到小时的短时效预报中表现不佳，因为该时间尺度下初始条件的精度主导预报质量。虽然卫星散射计观测已常规同化到NWP中，但尚未被直接用于预报，因此需要开发一种直接利用卫星观测的海上风短临预报方法。

Method: WindCastNet采用部分卷积长短期记忆（partial convolutional LSTM）网络，利用欧洲、中国和印度的散射计星座的微波雷达观测数据。方法中编码了空间观测掩码和观测间隔，并通过连续时间表示实现任意预报时效的预测，有效处理了卫星观测的不规则空间覆盖、异步采样和可变重访时间问题。

Result: 在北海区域评估中：1）相比HARMONIE MEPS模型，WindCastNet在1小时和2小时预报时效分别降低RMSE 23%和7%；2）在前3小时预报中相比持续性预报方法提升9-15%；3）在强风条件和空间非均匀流场下预报技能下降。

Conclusion: 卫星散射计星座可为短期海上风预报提供独立且具有竞争力的来源，不仅为可再生能源预报开辟新机会，还可推广到更广泛的海洋天气应用，如热带气旋短临预报。

Abstract: Accurate intraday forecasts of offshore wind are becoming increasingly important for power system operation and the integration of growing shares of offshore wind energy. Operational forecasts rely predominantly on numerical weather prediction (NWP), which is not optimized for lead times of minutes to hours, where initial-condition accuracy dominates forecast skill. Although satellite scatterometer observations are routinely assimilated into NWP, they have not previously been used directly for forecasting. Here we present WindCastNet, the first satellite-based nowcasting framework for offshore wind speed and direction, introducing a new paradigm for intraday forecasting that learns from spatiotemporally irregular satellite observations. WindCastNet predicts offshore wind fields from observations acquired by satellite scatterometer constellations. WindCastNet employs a partial convolutional long short-term memory network that exploits microwave radar observations from the European, Chinese, and Indian scatterometers despite their irregular spatial coverage, asynchronous sampling, and variable revisit times. Spatial observation masks and inter-observation intervals are encoded, while a continuous temporal representation enables forecasts at arbitrary lead times. Evaluated over the North Sea, WindCastNet reduces the root-mean-square error by 23% and 7% relative to the HARMONIE MEPS model at lead times of 1 and 2 h, respectively, and outperforms persistence by 9-15% during the first three forecast hours. Forecast skill decreases under strong-wind conditions and spatially non-uniform flow. These results demonstrate that satellite scatterometer constellations can provide an independent and competitive source of short-term offshore wind forecasts, opening new opportunities for renewable energy forecasting but also broader marine weather applications, including tropical cyclone nowcasting.

</details>


### [199] [When Do Learned Diffusion Proposals Help Constraint Solving? A Controlled Study on Continuous Algebraic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27169)
*Quang Bui,Sparsh Roy,Akash Gundimeda,Davin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARC系统，将代数约束系统转化为因子图，使用图神经网络扩散去噪器进行赋值提议，结合精确计算机代数优化和符号验证。研究发现，在相同的优化预算下，学习驱动的提议方法仅在特定高维场景中优于随机多起点搜索，而在大多数实际系统中并无优势。作者给出了随机多起点成功的精确概率公式，并划定了学习提议能够改进求解器的适用区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续代数约束系统求解需要同时解决两个问题：值分配和结构增广。经典求解器对第一个问题有效，但对第二个问题只能通过枚举解决。本文旨在探索学习驱动的提议方法在何种条件下能够超越传统的随机多起点搜索。

Method: 将约束系统转化为因子图，使用图神经网络扩散去噪器生成赋值提议，然后通过精确的计算机代数能量优化进行精炼，最后由精确符号检查器验证解的正确性。同时设计了随机多起点搜索作为基线对比。

Result: 在候选条件修复排序任务中，学习方法的平衡非线性菜单准确率达0.997，远优于随机的0.236。但在赋值决策方面，学习方法仅在低维耦合场景中与随机重启持平，在高维非耦合场景有优势，一旦变量耦合优势消失。在8个真实世界系统中，经典多起点方法全部求解成功，而无一处于学习方法的有利区间。

Conclusion: 学习驱动的提议方法在代数约束求解中的优势仅限于特定的高维非耦合场景，在大多数实际系统中并不优于经典的随机多起点搜索。论文划定了学习提议能够改进求解器的适用区间，为未来研究提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Solving a continuous algebraic constraint system requires two decisions: which values satisfy the constraints, and which structural augmentation renders an unsolvable system solvable. Classical solvers answer the first well and the second only by enumeration. On that discrete decision, a candidate-conditioned repair ranker choosing among K augmentations reaches the exhaustive-search ceiling at a fraction of the calls, outperforming random (0.997 vs 0.236 balanced nonlinear menu accuracy; p < 10^-70; 0.982 +/- 0.006 across seeds) and beating a budget-matched per-candidate probe on accuracy and cost. MARC turns such a system into a factor graph, over which a graph-neural diffusion denoiser proposes assignments, descent on an exact computer-algebra energy polishes them, and an exact symbolic checker certifies solutions. Evaluations of diffusion-based proposals rarely include one control: random multi-start under the same refinement budget. Applied to our system, it sharply curtails what the learned proposal contributes on the value decision. Does it beat random multi-start at choosing satisfying assignments? Only narrowly, in a predictable regime. Across trapped low-dimensional families it ties with random restart, but dominates in high dimension, where random search fails. Once variables couple, the advantage is gone. Since all methods share one polish and one checker, best-of-K random multi-start succeeds with probability exactly 1 - (1 - q(n))^K, where q(n) is single-start reachability; one measured constant, with no free parameters, reproduces the entire curve (mean absolute error 0.012). The favorable regime is not specific to our synthetic families: across eight real-world systems in robotics, positioning, optimization, and algebra, classical multi-start solved all eight, none in the learning-favorable regime. We map the regimes in which learned proposals improve solvers.

</details>


### [200] [Inverse Learning of Latent Risk-Neutral Densities from Irregular Option Quotes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27188)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Michael Galarnyk,Aadi Dash,Nicholas A. Welsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明期权定价精度不等于隐含风险中性密度恢复精度，通过合成和真实市场基准测试发现：双组分对数正态混合模型在综合误差上最优，但不同学习器各有专长（如DeepONet在特定分位数和方差误差上表现突出）；数值条件分析表明定价方向存在大量冗余；验证集自适应可提升DeepONet效果，但混合模型和SVI在真实NIFTY数据上仍更准确。结论是应依据目标选择模型，不存在通用最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究常假设期权定价准确即隐含密度恢复准确，本文旨在揭示二者之间存在显著差异，并系统量化这种差距。

Method: 设计两个互补基准：控制合成基准（已知真实密度）和NIFTY真实市场基准；比较多种模型（对数正态混合、DeepONet、quote transformer、SVI等）；进行数值条件分析评估定价方向的奇异性。

Result: 混合模型综合误差最小；DeepONet在1%分位数和方差误差上分别降低39.0%和34.6%；quote transformer在Merton族上L1降低16.4%；95/126定价方向数值为零；两个密度L1=0.061时产生相同价格；测试时自适应降低DeepONet RMSE 28.3%，但混合和SVI仍更准。

Conclusion: 不存在通用最优模型，应依据具体任务目标选择具有相应归纳偏好的模型。

Abstract: Accurate option prices do not imply accurate recovery of the latent risk-neutral density. We study this distinction with two complementary benchmarks. A controlled benchmark exposes simulator-truth densities for latent evaluation, while a chronological NIFTY benchmark tests only held-out market prices. A two-component lognormal mixture has the lowest aggregate price, $L^1$, Wasserstein, and fixed-tail errors on the synthetic benchmark. Learned operators retain narrower strengths: DeepONet reduces 1% quantile and variance error by 39.0% and 34.6% relative to the mixture, and a quote transformer reduces $L^1$ by 16.4% on the structurally misspecified Merton family. A numerical conditioning analysis explains why these rankings can differ: after enforcing mass and forward constraints, 95 of 126 pricing directions are numerically null, and two densities separated by $L^1 = 0.061$ produce identical prices on the covered strikes. On 524 held-out NIFTY calls, validation-selected test-time adaptation reduces DeepONet RMSE by 28.3%, but per-expiry mixture and SVI fits remain much more accurate. The evidence supports target-dependent inductive bias, not a universal winner.

</details>


### [201] [From Classification to Regression: Using a Fruitfly to Solve Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27196)
*Shady E. Ahmed,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受果蝇感知机制启发，提出一种用分类方法解决回归任务的通用框架，通过局部模式库替代复杂全局代理模型，在非线性动力学、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习等任务中实现高效预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归方法依赖复杂全局代理模型，计算和存储开销大；而科学数据往往在输入空间中呈现有限且重复的模式。受果蝇高效感知环境的生物机制启发，希望用局部模式匹配的方式替代全局建模，降低计算和内存需求。

Method: 构建有限局部模式库，通过测量查询与存储模式之间的相似度，对关联响应进行加权重构得到预测结果。采用离线-在线工作流：离线阶段从数据或控制方程中提取模式，在线阶段仅需相似度评估和响应聚合。

Result: 该方法在非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习等任务上有效，能够降低计算和内存需求，并显式控制精度、存储和推理成本之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 受果蝇启发的局部模式回归框架是一种高效、灵活的非线性输入输出关系学习方法，尤其适用于数据空间有限且重复的科学计算场景，能在精度与效率之间取得可控平衡。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to regression tasks using classification which is motivated by the mechanism used by fruitflies to sense their environment. Specifically, we formulate a general framework for learning nonlinear input-output relationships by replacing complex global surrogate models with a finite library of representative local patterns. Since scientific data often occupy limited and recurring regions of the input space, we generate predictions by measuring similarities between a query and stored patterns, then combining their associated responses through weighted reconstruction. We apply this approach to nonlinear dynamical systems, data-driven regression, and physics-informed learning using suitable embeddings and similarity measures. For dynamical systems, our offline-online workflow extracts patterns from data or governing equations during the offline phase, while online prediction requires only similarity evaluation and response aggregation. This structure helps us reduce computational and memory demands while providing explicit control over the trade-off among accuracy, storage, and inference cost.

</details>


### [202] [Do You Really Need to Pretrain Q-Functions for Online RL Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27203)
*Perry Dong,Ron Polonsky,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Fin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了值函数预训练在基于价值强化学习中的有效性，发现传统的Q函数预训练往往不如随机初始化有效，并提出了一种名为"策略集成初始化"(IPE)的简单方法来提升在线微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在预训练策略的基础上进行强化学习微调时，预训练Q函数是否真的有助于提升性能，以及为什么现有的naive Q函数预训练方法效果不佳。

Method: 提出Initialization via Policy Ensemble (IPE)方法，通过训练多个多样化策略并利用它们的联合轨迹来引导在线强化学习中的Q函数学习。

Result: IPE在一系列具有挑战性的连续控制基准测试中，相比于naive Q函数预训练，平均提升了1.26倍的微调性能。

Conclusion: naive Q函数预训练效果有限的原因是预训练阶段学到的Q函数与在线微调收敛到的Q函数之间存在根本性不匹配，而IPE通过策略集成初始化有效缓解了这一问题。

Abstract: Pre-training followed by fine-tuning has become the dominant recipe for learning performant policies, and in value-based reinforcement learning (RL) this raises a natural question: given a pretrained policy, should the Q-function be pretrained on offline data too? Conventional wisdom suggests it should, but recent results show that online RL with a randomly-initialized Q-function can result in highly performant and reliable policies without needing to pretrain the Q-function. In this paper, we systematically study whether pretraining the Q-function actually helps when fine-tuning on top of a pretrained base policy. We find, surprisingly, that naive Q-function pretraining often provides little benefit over random initialization. We show this stems from a fundamental mismatch: the Q-function learned during pretraining targets the pretrained policy's Q-function, not the Q-function that online fine-tuning converges to, and this gap persists even after offline value maximization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Initialization via Policy Ensemble (IPE), a simple method that trains multiple diverse policies and uses their pooled rollouts to bootstrap the Q-function learning in online RL. Across a suite of challenging continuous control benchmarks, IPE yields an average 1.26x improvement in fine-tuning performance over naive Q-function pre-training.

</details>
